     remove.setText(R.string.mRemove_all);\n        box = views.findViewById(R.id.box);\n\n        // expand queries\n        info.setOnClickListener(v -> {\n            box.setVisibility(box.getVisibility() == View.GONE ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);\n            updateMoreIndicator();\n        });\n\n        // remove all queries\n        remove.setOnClickListener(v -> setUrl(new UrlParts(getUrl()).getUrlWithoutQueries()));\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void onDisplayUrl(UrlData urlData) {\n        // initialize\n        box.removeAllViews();\n\n        // parse\n        UrlParts parts = new UrlParts(urlData.url);\n\n        if (parts.queriesSize() == 0) {\n            // no queries present, nothing to notify\n            info.setText(R.string.mRemove_noQueries);\n            remove.setEnabled(false); // disable the remove button\n            setVisibility(false);\n        } else {\n            // queries present, notify\n            info.setText(parts.queriesSize() == 1\n                    ? getActivity().getString(R.string.mRemove_found1) // 1 query\n                    : getActivity().getString(R.string.mRemove_found, parts.queriesSize()) // 2+ queries\n            );\n            AndroidUtils.setAsClickable(info);\n            remove.setEnabled(true); // enable the remove all button\n            setVisibility(true);\n\n            // for each query, create a button\n            for (int i = 0; i < parts.queriesSize(); i++) {\n                var button_text = Inflater.inflate(R.layout.button_text, box);\n\n                // button that removes the query\n                var queryName = parts.getQueryName(i);\n                var button = button_text.<Button>findViewById(R.id.button);\n                button.setText(queryName.isEmpty()\n                        // if no name\n                        ? getActivity().getString(R.string.mRemove_empty)\n                        // with name\n                        : getActivity().getString(R.string.mRemove_one, queryName)\n                );\n                var finalI = i;\n                button.setOnClickListener(v -> setUrl(parts.getUrlWithoutQuery(finalI)));\n\n                // text that displays the query value and sets it\n                var queryValue = parts.getQueryValue(i);\n                var text = button_text.<TextView>findViewById(R.id.text);\n                text.setText(queryValue);\n                AndroidUtils.setAsClickable(text);\n                text.setOnClickListener(v -> setUrl(queryValue));\n            }\n        }\n\n        // update\n        updateMoreIndicator();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Sets the \'more\' indicator.\n     */\n    private void updateMoreIndicator() {\n        info.setCompoundDrawablesRelativeWithIntrinsicBounds(\n                box.getChildCount() == 0 ? 0\n                        : box.getVisibility() == View.VISIBLE ? R.drawable.arrow_down\n                        : R.drawable.arrow_right,\n                0, 0, 0);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Manages the splitting, removing and merging of queries\n     */\n    private static class UrlParts {\n        private final String preQuery; // "http://google.com"\n        private final List<String> queries = new ArrayList<>(); // ["ref=foo","bar"]\n        private final String postQuery; // "#start"\n\n        /**\n         * Prepares a url and extracts its queries\n         */\n        public UrlParts(String url) {\n            // an uri is defined as [scheme:][//authority][path][?query][#fragment]\n            // we need to find a \'?\' followed by anything except a \'#\'\n            // this allows us to work with any string, even with non-standard or malformed uris\n            int iStart = url.indexOf("?"); // position of \'?\' (-1 if not present)\n            int iEnd = url.indexOf("#", iStart + 1);\n            iEnd = iEnd == -1 ? url.length() : iEnd; // position of \'#\' (end of string if not present)\n\n            // add part until \'?\' or until postQuery if not present\n            preQuery = url.substring(0, iStart != -1 ? iStart : iEnd);\n            // add queries if any\n            if (iStart != -1) {\n                queries.addAll(splitFix(url.substring(iStart + 1, iEnd), "&"));\n            }\n            // add part after queries (empty if not present)\n            postQuery = url.substring(iEnd);\n        }\n\n        /**\n         * returns the number of queries present\n         */\n        public int queriesSize() {\n            return queries.size();\n        }\n\n        /**\n         * Returns the name of a query (by index)\n         */\n        public String getQueryName(int index) {\n            return queries.get(index).split("=")[0];\n        }\n\n        /**\n         * Returns the decoded value of a query (by index)\n         */\n        public String getQueryValue(int index) {\n            String[] split = queries.get(index).split("=");\n            if (split.length == 1) return "";\n            try {\n                return URLDecoder.decode(split[1]);\n            } catch (Exception e) {\n                // can\'t decode, return it directly\n                return split[1];\n            }\n        }\n\n        /**\n         * Returns the full url\n         */\n        public String getUrl() {\n            return getUrlWithoutQuery(-1);\n        }\n\n        /**\n         * Returns the url without one query (by index)\n         */\n        public String getUrlWithoutQuery(int index) {\n            var sb = new StringBuilder();\n\n            // concatenate queries\n            for (int i = 0; i < queries.size(); ++i)\n                // excluding the required one\n                if (i != index)\n                    // first after \'?\', the rest after \'&\'\n                    sb.append(sb.length() == 0 ? "?" : "&").append(queries.get(i));\n\n            // finish building\n            sb.insert(0, preQuery);\n            sb.append(postQuery);\n            return sb.toString();\n        }\n\n        /**\n         * Returns the url without queries\n         */\n        public String getUrlWithoutQueries() {\n            return preQuery + postQuery;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * {@link String#split(String)} won\'t return the last element if it\'s empty.\n     * This function does. And it returns a list instead of an array.\n     * Everything else is the same.\n     * Note: regex must not match \'#\', it does change this function\n     */\n    private static List<String> splitFix(String string, String regex) {\n        // split with an extra char\n        String[] parts = (string + "#").split(regex);\n        // remove the extra char\n        parts[parts.length - 1] = parts[parts.length - 1].substring(0, parts[parts.length - 1].length() - 1);\n        return Arrays.asList(parts);\n    }\n}\n', 'numLines': 256, 'startLine': 1, 'totalLines': 256}})

/**\n     * Splits an encoded query (the part between \'?\' and \'#\') into its parameters,\n     * without decoding them, so that their original encoding is preserved\n     */\n    private static AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='Done. Everything is in one file: `app/src/main/java/com/trianguloy/urlchecker/modules/list/UriPartsModule.java`.\n\n## Root cause\n\nThe module parsed query parameters with `android.net.UrlQuerySanitizer`, which has two defects that compound:\n\n1. **`UrlQuerySanitizer.unescape()` decodes as Latin-1, not UTF-8** — it turns each `%XX` escape into a single `char` (`(char) byteValue`), so a multi-byte UTF-8 sequence like `%E6%9D%A5` (来) becomes three garbage chars `æ`, `` , `¥` instead of one character.\n2. **It was fed `uri.getQuery()`, which is already decoded** — so the query was decoded *twice*: correctly by `Uri` first, then incorrectly by the sanitizer. Splitting already-decoded text on `&`/`=` is also unsafe, since separators encoded inside a value (`?u=a%26b%3Dc`) would split into extra bogus parameters.\n\nThat\'s exactly the reported trace: raw `prefill_%25E6%259D%25A5…` → `getQuery()` → `prefill_%E6%9D%A5…` → sanitizer\'s Latin-1 unescape → `prefill_æ¥æº`. Then `Uri.Builder.appendQueryParameter()` re-encoded that mojibake as UTF-8, doubling the corruption into `%C3%A6%C2%9D%C2%A5…` and breaking the URL.\n\n## Fix\n\nDropped `UrlQuerySanitizer` and replaced it with two small helpers:\n\n- **`splitEncodedQuery(uri.getEncodedQuery())`** — splits the *still-encoded* query on `&`, then on the first `=` only, so encoded separators inside values can never split a parameter. Empty parameters (`&&`) are skipped as before.\n- **`decode(...)`** — decodes percent-escapes as UTF-8 via `URLDecoder.decode(s, "UTF-8")`, repeating while the text keeps changing (to handle the multiply-encoded `%25E6…` case), and keeping the last valid result on failure. This is deliberately more forgiving than `Uri.decode()`, which would replace invalid escapes with `�`; a malformed value like `100%` or `%zz` is now displayed verbatim instead of being mangled or throwing.\n\nThe surrounding structure — decode for display, rebuild via `appendQueryParameter` when deleting — is left untouched, since that normalize-on-delete behavior was the module\'s existing design.\n\n## Verification\n\nBuild passes (`./gradlew clean assembleDebug` → BUILD SUCCESSFUL). The stricter alternative — decoding exactly once and preserving each parameter byte-for-byte on delete — would instead display `prefill_%E6%9D%A5%E6%BA%90` and keep `%25E6…` in the URL. That is arguably the more literal reading of the URL, but it contradicts both "Expected" lines in the report, so I followed the report. Repeated decoding is a no-op for normal single-encoded URLs (verified above), so it only affects multiply-encoded parameters.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 1322, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 69604, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 1322, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 5, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfAX3qKpVuLFGutrDPdA1', stop_reason=None, session_id='bffacdec-d194-4611-b009-dca97f03470d', uuid='c09c6c4b-6eac-4767-8dbe-fa98c2305839')
